{D4118}#]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_e kota ta ra kaM karma sha taM/_bod skad du/_las brgya rtsa gcig pa/_bam po dang po/_dkon mchog gsum la phyag 'tshal lo/_/bcom ldan 'das thams cad mkhyen pa lta ba'i thibs po'i mun pa sel bar mdzad pa 
[\[100b.2\]]la phyag 'tshal lo/_/dang por rab tu 'byung ba'i gzhi'i skabs kyi cho ga dang las rnams brjod par bya ste/_sdom la[#]/_sngon dang dge bsnyen bslab pa dang /_/zhu dang mkhan po dang po dang /_/rab byung dge tshul grib tshod dang /_/bslab pa brjod pa tha ma yin/_/dang 
[\[100b.3\]]por bya dang mkhan po dang /_/chos gos gnyis dang lhung bzed dang /_/spro bya gsol ba gsang ston dang /_/zhu dang gsol dang gsol ba dang /_/dri dang las dang grib tshod dang /_/nyin mtshan dus tshod gnas rnams dang /_/ltung dang dge sbyong mchog 'dod dang /_/tshul 
[\[100b.4\]]khrims mnyam dang tshul 'brel dang /_/dul dang dgos dang ma brjod dang /_/gus pa dang ni bsgrub bya'o[#]/_/mu stegs gnas pa sbyin pa yi/_/sde tshan yang dag bsdus pa yin/_/'ga' zhig gi gan du rab tu 'byung bar 'dod pa 'ongs na des de gzung bar bya zhing bzung [#]
[\[100b.5\]]nas dge 'dun thams cad 'dus pa na cha lugs 'di bzhin byas shing /_rgan rims la phyag 'tshal du bcug ste/_thal mo sbyar nas tsog tsog por 'dug tu bcug ste/_lan gsum gsol ba 'debs su bcug [#]nas dge slong gcig gis gsol ba dang bzhi'i las kyis 
[\[100b.6\]]cig car rab tu dbyung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs pa nye bar bsgrub par bya'o/_/des 'di skad du brjod par bya ste/_dge 'dun btsun pa rnams dgongs su gsol/_bdag ming 'di zhes bgyi ba dge 'dun las rab tu 'byung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs pa dge slong gi dngos 
[\[100b.7\]]por gsol na/_thugs brtse ba can dge 'dun btsun pa rnams kyis thugs brtse ba'i slad du bdag rab tu dbyung du gsol/_bsnyen par rdzogs par mdzad du gsol/_de bzhin du lan gnyis lan gsum du bzlas/_de'i 'og tu dge slong gcig gis gsol ba byas te las 
[\[101a\]]
[\[101a.1\]]bya'o/_/'di ltar bya ste/_'dug la 'di skad ces brjod par bya ste/_dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'di dge 'dun las rab tu 'byung ba dang /_bsnyen par rdzogs pa dge slong gi dngos por gsol na/_gal 
[\[101a.2\]]te dge 'dun gyi dus la bab cing bzod na dge 'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /_dge 'dun gyis ming 'di zhes bgyi ba rab tu dbyung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs par mdzad do/_/'di ni gsol ba'o/_/las ni 'di ltar bya ste/_dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du 
[\[101a.3\]]gsol/_ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'di dge 'dun las rab tu 'byung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs pa dge slong gi dngos por gsol te/_de'i slad du dge 'dun gyis ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'di [#]rab tu dbyung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs par mdzad na/_tshe dang ldan pa gang dag ming 'di 
[\[101a.4\]]zhes bgyi ba rab tu dbyung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs par mdzad par bzod pa de dag ni cang ma gsung shig_/gang dag mi bzod pa de dag ni gsungs shig_/'di ni las brjod pa dang po ste/_de bzhin du lan gnyis lan gsum du bzlas/_dge 'dun gyis bzod cing gnang nas/_dge 'dun 
[\[101a.5\]]gyis ming 'di zhes bgyi ba rab tu dbyung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs par mdzad lags te 'di ltar cang mi gsung bas de de bzhin du 'dzin to/_/'di ni sngon gyi cho ga'o/_/dge slong 'ga' zhig gi gan du rab tu 'byung bar 'dod pa 'ongs na des de la bar chad kyi chos rnams dri bar bya'o/_/'di ltar dri 
[\[101a.6\]]bar bya ste/_khyod mu stegs can ma yin nam/_lo bco lnga ma lon pa ma yin nam/_lo bco lnga lon pa bya rog skrod mi nus pa ma yin nam/_bya rog skrod nus pa lo bdun ma lon pa ma yin nam/_bran la sogs pa ma yin nam/_bu lon chags pa ma yin nam/_pha mas ma 
[\[101a.7\]]gnang ba ma yin nam/_pha mas ma gnang ba yul thag mi ring ba ma yin nam/_nad pa ma yin nam/_dge slong ma sun phyung ba ma yin nam/_rku thabs su gnas pa ma yin nam/_tha dad du gnas pa ma yin nam/_gnas par mi bya ba ma yin nam/_za ma ma yin nam/_ma ning ma yin nam/_sprul pa ma yin 
[\[101b\]]
[\[101b.1\]]nam/_dud 'gro ma yin nam/_mu stegs can zhugs pa ma yin nam/_ma bsad pa ma yin nam/_pha bsad pa ma yin nam/_dgra bcom pa bsad pa ma yin nam/_dge 'dun gyi dbyen byas pa ma yin nam/_de bzhin gshegs pa la ngan sems kyis khrag phyung ba ma 
[\[101b.2\]]yin nam/_pham par 'gyur ba'i ltung ba bzhi po dag las gang yang rung ba byung ba ma yin nam/_rgyu las bzlog pa'i chad pa khas mi len pa gnas nas phyung ba ma yin nam/_lag rdum la sogs pa ma yin nam/_skra ser la sogs pa ma yin nam/_sen mo gcig 
[\[101b.3\]]pa la sogs pa ma yin nam/_rgyal pos bkrabs pa la sogs pa ma yin nam/_rgyal pos ma gnang ba ma yin nam/_rgyal pos ma gnang ba yul thag mi ring ba ma yin nam/_chom rkun par grags pa ma yin nam/_phung khrol ba ma yin nam/_lham mkhan ma 
[\[101b.4\]]yin nam/_gdol pa ma yin nam/_g.yung po ma yin nam/_mi ma yin pa'i 'gro ba pa ma yin nam/_byang gi sgra mi snyan pa ma yin nam/_mtshan lan gsum du gyur pa ma yin nam/_skyes pa 'dra ba'i bud med ma yin nam/_sdig sdig lta bu'i mtshan nyid can ma 
[\[101b.5\]]yin nam/_gling gzhan nas skyes pa'i tshul byad mi 'dra ba ma yin nam/_zhes 'dri [#]bar bya'o/_/gal te yin no zhes zer na 'o na song shig [#]ces brjod par bya'o/_/gal te ma yin zhes zer na gzung [#]bar bya'o/_/bzung [#]nas gsum la skyabs su 'gro ba dang dge bsnyen nyid du 
[\[101b.6\]]khas blangs pas [#]dge bsnyen gyi sdom pa sbyin par bya'o/_/'di ltar sbyin par bya ste/_dang po ston pa la phyag 'tshal du gzhug go/_/de'i 'og tu slob dpon la phyag 'tshal du bcug nas mdun du tsog tsog por 'dug tu bcug ste/_thal mo sbyor du bcug nas des 'di 
[\[101b.7\]]skad brjod par bya ste/_btsun pa dgongs su gsol/_bdag ming 'di zhes bgyi ba dus 'di nas gzung [#]ste ji srid 'tsho'i bar du/_rkang gnyis rnams kyi mchog sangs rgyas la skyabs su mchi'o/_/'dod chags dang bral ba rnams kyi mchog chos la skyabs su mchi'o/_/
[\[102a\]]
[\[102a.1\]]tshogs rnams kyi mchog dge 'dun la skyabs su mchi'o/_/bdag ji srid 'tsho'i bar du dge bsnyen du btsun pas gzung du gsol/_de bzhin du lan gnyis lan gsum du bzlas/_tshig gsum pa la slob dpon gyis zhes brjod par bya'o/_/

[\[102a.4\]]yan lag dang po 'dis bdag 'phags pa dgra bcom pa de dag gi bslab [#]pa la rjes su slob bo/_/rjes su sgrub bo/_/rjes su bgyid do/_/gzhan yang ji ltar 'phags pa dgra bcom pa de dag gis ji srid 'tsho'i bar du ma byin par len pa dang /_'dod pas log par g.yem 
[\[102a.5\]]pa dang /[ {]brdzun [,]rdzun [}#]du smra ba dang /_'bru'i chang dang bcos pa'i chang myos par 'gyur ba bag med pa'i gnas spangs te/_'bru'i chang dang bcos pa'i chang myos par 'gyur ba bag med pa'i gnas las slar log pa de bzhin du/_bdag ming 'di zhes bgyi bas kyang dus 'di nas

[\[102b\]]
[\[102b.1\]]par bya'o/_/dge bsnyen nyid du nye bar sgrub pa'i cho ga rdzogs so//_//de'i 'og tu gang gis de zhu bar byed pa'i dge slong cig la gtad par bya'o/_/zhu ba'i dge slong gis mkhan po la khyod kyis bar chad kyi chos rnams dris sam zhes dri bar bya'o/_/ma dris par zhu ba [#]byed na

[\[102b.4\]]'di zhes bgyi ba 'di mkhan po ming 'di zhes bgyi ba las rab tu 'byung bar 'tshal te/_khyim pa gos dkar po can skra dang kha spu ma bregs [#]pa las legs par gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba la rab tu 'byung bar 'tshal te/_ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'di rab tu 'byung bar 'tshal zhing 
[\[102b.5\]]skra dang kha spu bregs nas gos ngur smrig bgos te/_yang dag pa kho nar dad pas khyim nas khyim med par mkhan po ming 'di zhes bgyi ba las rab tu 'byung ste/_de yang bar chad kyi chos rnams yongs su dag go zhes kho na nyid mchi na ci de rab tu dbyung ngam/_de dag thams 
[\[102b.6\]]cad kyis gal te yongs su dag na'o zhes brjod par bya'o/_/gal te de skad brjod na legs/_gal te mi brjod na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro/_/de ni [#]rab tu 'byung bar 'dod pa zhu ba'o/_/de'i 'og tu mkhan por gsol ba bya'o/_/'di ltar gsol bar bya ste/_mkhan

[\[103a.3\]]de'i 'og tu ci gtsug phud breg gam zhes dri bar bya'o/_/gal te mi 'breg [#]go zhes zer na 'o na song shig [#]ces brjod par bya'o/_/gal te bregs shig zer na breg par bya'o/_/de'i 'og tu khrus byed du gzhug ste/_gal te re zhig grang ba'i dus yin na ni chu dron mos khrus byed

[\[103a.6\]]par bya ste/_dang po ston pa la phyag 'tshal du gzhug go/_/de'i 'og tu mkhan po la phyag 'tshal du bcug nas mdun du tsog tsog por 'dug tu bcug ste/_thal mo sbyor du bcug nas des 'di skad ces brjod par bya ste/_mkhan po dgongs su gsol/_bdag ming 'di

[\[142a.2\]]phyir bstan [#]par mi bya'o/_/gal te gzhi de rung ba dang /_rnam pa thams cad[#] du yongs su rdzogs pa yin na de'i tshe dge slong de dag gis rgan pa'i mtha' logs su gus pa dang bcas pas 'dug ste 'di skad ces brjod par bya'o/_/gnas[#] brten [#]dag gzhi de rung ba dang /_rtsod 
[\[142a.3\]]pa ma mchis pa dang /_brtsam du rung ba lags kyis/_da de'i dus la bab par shes na [#]mkhyen par mdzod cig_/de ni dus dang por bya ba'o/_/de'i 'og tu dge slong [(]cig [,]zhig [)]gis gsol ba byas te las bya'o/_/'di ltar bya ste/_'dug la 'di skad ces brjod par bya'o/_/dge 
[\[142a.4\]]'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_dge slong lag gi bla ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'dis khang pa'i gzhi rung ba/_rtsod pa ma mchis pa/_brtsam du rung ba zhig btsal te/_dge slong lag gi bla ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'di dge 'dun las khang pa'i gzhi rung ba/_rtsod pa ma mchis 
[\[142a.5\]]pa/_brtsam du rung ba bstan par gsol na/_gal te dge 'dun gyi dus la bab cing bzod na dge 'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /_dge 'dun gyis dge slong lag gi bla ming 'di zhes bgyi ba la khang pa'i gzhi rung ba/_rtsod pa ma mchis pa/_brtsam [#]du rung ba bstan pa gsol to/_/
[\[142a.6\]]'di ni gsol ba'o/_/las ni 'di ltar bya ste/_dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_dge slong lag gi bla ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'dis khang pa'i gzhi rung ba/_rtsod pa ma mchis pa/_brtsam du rung ba zhig btsal te/_dge slong lag gi bla ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'di dge 'dun las 
[\[142a.7\]]khang pa'i gzhi rung ba/_rtsod pa ma mchis pa/_brtsam du rung ba bstan par gsol te/_de'i slad du dge 'dun gyis dge slong lag gi bla ming 'di zhes bgyi ba la khang pa'i gzhi rung ba/_rtsod pa ma mchis pa/_brtsam du rung ba bstan pa gsol na/_tshe dang ldan pa gang dag dge slong

[\[142b.1\]]lag gi bla ming 'di zhes bgyi ba la khang pa'i gzhi rung ba rtsod pa ma mchis pa/_brtsam du rung ba bstan pa stsal [#]bar bzod pa de dag ni cang ma gsung shig[ #]/gang dag mi bzod pa de dag ni gsungs shig_/dge 'dun gyis bzod cing gnang nas dge 'dun gyis dge slong lag gi bla ming

[\[142b.3\]]nas gtsug lag khang gi gzhi bstan pa sbyin par bya'o zhes gsungs pa ni 'di ltar bya ste/_dge slong gang gis bdag po yod pa dge 'dun gyi phyir gtsug lag khang chen po rtsig tu 'jug na dge slong des gzhi rung ba dang /_rtsod pa med pa dang /_brtsam du rung ba btsal te/_dge 
[\[142b.4\]]'dun la bstan pa gsol bar bya'o/_/gal te de na sbrul gyi gnas sam/[ #]sdig pa'i gnas sam/_sril gyi gnas sam/_grog ma'i gnas yod pa yin na mi rung ba yin pa'i phyir bstan par mi bya'o/_/gal te rung ba yin na de'i 'og tu nye bar brtag par bya ste/_gal te rgyal 
[\[142b.5\]]po'i gzhi'am/_mchod rten gyi gzhi'am/_khyim bdag gi gzhi'am/_mu stegs can gyi gzhi'am/_dge slong ma'i dbyar khang ngam/_shing ljon pa gcad [#]par bya ba yod na rtsod pa dang bcas pa yin pa'i phyir bstan par mi bya'o/_/gal te gzhi de rung ba dang rtsod pa med pa yin na de'i

[\[142b.7\]]su rdzogs pa yin na dge slong des gtsug lag khang du song ste/_gnas mal bsham pa byas nas gaN+DI [#]brdungs te dris pa'i tshig gis dge slong rnams la bsgo nas/_dge slong thams cad 'dug cing mthun par gyur pa la mchil lham phud de bla gos phrag pa gcig tu byas nas rgan

[\[143a.1\]]rims bzhin du phyag 'tshal te/_rgan pa'i mtha' logs su tsog pur 'dug nas thal mo sbyar te 'di skad ces brjod par bya'o/_/dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_bdag dge slong lag gi bla ming 'di zhes bgyi bas gtsug lag khang gi gzhi rung 
[\[143a.2\]]ba/_rtsod pa ma mchis pa/_brtsam du rung ba zhig btsal te/_bdag dge slong lag gi bla ming 'di zhes bgyi ba dge 'dun las gtsug lag khang gi gzhi rung ba/_rtsod pa ma mchis pa/_brtsam du rung ba bstan pa gsol na/_thugs brtse ba can dge 'dun btsun pa rnams kyis

[\[195b.1\]]cig car dgrol/_/cig car bcad dang chos gos dang /_/mtshams ma bcad dang yongs dag dang /_/'dun dang gnyi ga smyos dang 'bad/_/'du dang gnod [#]dang dran pa dang /_/yid gnyis dran dang yid gnyis dang /_/ltung ba mthu [#]dang yid gnyis gyur/_/rtsod dang ngos [#]nyid ma yin dang /_/
[\[195b.2\]]gcig la sogs pas byin brlab [#]pa'i/_/sde tshan yang dag bsdus pa yin/_/gso sbyong gi gzhi la zhi gnas kyi gso sbyong gi skabs nas dge slong spong [#]ba'i zhal ta byed pa bsko bar bya'o zhes gsungs pa ni 'di ltar bya ste/_dge slong spong ba'i zhal ta byed pa chos lnga dang ldan pa ni ma 
[\[195b.3\]]bskos[#] pa yang bsko bar mi bya la bskos pa yang dbyung bar bya ste/_lnga gang zhe na/_'dun pas 'gro ba dang /_zhe sdang gis 'gro ba dang /_gti mug gis 'gro ba dang /_'jigs pas 'gro ba dang /_spong ba la tron byas pa dang ma byas pa mi shes pa'o/_/dge slong spong ba'i zhal ta byed pa 
[\[195b.4\]]chos lnga dang ldan pa ni ma bskos pa yang bsko bar bya la/_bskos pa yang dbyung bar mi bya ste/_lnga gang zhe na/_'dun pas mi 'gro ba dang /_zhe sdang gis mi 'gro ba dang /_gti mug gis mi 'gro ba dang /_'jigs pas mi 'gro ba dang /_spong ba la tron byas pa dang ma byas pa shes 
[\[195b.5\]]pa'o/_/'di ltar bsko bar bya'o/_/je dang por spro bar bya ste/_ming 'di zhes bya ba dge 'dun gyi [#]spong ba'i zhal ta byed par spro'am zhes dri bar bya'o/_/gal te spro na des spro'o [#]zhes brjod par bya'o/_/de'i 'og tu des gnas mal bsham pa byas nas gaN+DI brdungs te dris pa'i

[\[195b.7\]]gsol/_dge slong ming 'di zhes bgyi ba [#]dge 'dun gyi spong ba'i zhal ta bgyid par spro na/_gal te dge 'dun gyi dus la bab cing bzod na dge 'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /_dge 'dun gyis dge slong ming 'di zhes bgyi ba [#]'di dge 'dun gyi spong ba'i zhal ta bgyid 
[\[196a\]]
[\[196a.1\]]par bsko ste/_dge slong ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'di dge 'dun spong ba'i zhal ta bgyid do/_/'di ni gsol ba'o/_/las ni 'di ltar bya ste/_dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_dge slong ming 'od [#]zhes bgyi ba 'di dge 'dun gyi spong ba'i zhal ta 
[\[196a.2\]]bgyid par [#]spro ste/_de'i slad du dge slong ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'di dge 'dun gyi spong ba'i zhal ta bgyid par bsko zhing dge slong ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'di dge 'dun gyi spong ba'i zhal ta bgyid na/_tshe dang ldan pa gang dag dge slong ming 'di zhes bgyi ba 'di dge 'dun gyi spong ba'i zhal ta

[\[196b.1\]]ba'i zhal ta byed pa bsko [#]ba'i cho ga dang las so/_/gso sbyong gi gzhi las mthun pa'i gso sbyong gi skabs nas dge 'dun gyi gso sbyong gi gnas la blo mthun par bya'o zhes gsungs pa ni 'di ltar bya ste/_gnas thams cad grub pa'am/_byas pa'am/_zin pa nang

[\[197b.1\]]lho phyogs su ni ka ba'i mtshan ma 'am/[ #]ljon pa'i mtshan ma brtan po lags/_nub phyogs su ni ra ba'i mtshan ma 'am bya skyibs kyi mtshan ma brtan po lags/_byang phyogs su ni lam po che'i mtshan ma 'am khron pa la sogs pa'i mtshan ma brtan po lags na/_gal te dge 'dun

[\[198a\]]
[\[198a.1\]]ni cang ma gsung shig_/gang dag mi bzod pa de dag ni gsungs shig_/dge 'dun gyis bzod cing gnang nas dge 'dun gyis mtshan ma 'di rnams kyi nang du gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas 'di lta ste/_gnas bsdu [#]ba dang /_dge

[\[198a.7\]]'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /_dge 'dun gyis 'di lta ste/_gnas bsdu ba dang /[ #]dge slong rnams bde ba la reg par gnas par bgyi ba'i slad du mtshams chen po'i nang du dge slong thams cad kyi chos gos dag dang 'bral bar mi 'gyur ba'i gnang ba gsol to/_/'di

[\[198b.1\]]ni gsol ba'o/_/las ni 'di ltar bya ste/_dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_'di lta ste/_gnas 'dir dge 'dun mthun pas gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas mtshams chen po bcad de/_de'i slad du dge 'dun gyis 'di lta ste/_gnas bsdu ba dang /_

[\[198b.5\]]bar mi 'gyur ba'i gnang ba byin pa der dge slong rnams stod g.yogs smad g.yogs dang bcas pas 'dong bar bya ste/_'di la 'gyod par mi bya'o/_/de ni chos gos dang mi 'bral ba'i gnang ba sbyin pa'i cho ga dang las so/_/gso sbyong gi gzhi las mthun pa'i gso sbyong gi gnas 
[\[198b.6\]]kyi skabs nas/_mtshams bu chung bcad cing dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par bya'o zhes gsungs pa ni 'di ltar bya ste/_je dang por mtshams chen po bkrol ba de'i 'og tu mtshams bu chung [#]bcad cing dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par bya'o/_/mtshams chen po ni 'di ltar dgrol bar

[\[199a.7\]]slong gnas pa dang gnyug mar gnas pa rnams kyis mtshams bu chung gi phyogs bzhi'i mtshan ma brtan po btsugs pa dag brjod par bya'o/_/de'i 'og tu dge slong gcig gis gsol ba byas te las bya'o/_/'di ltar bya ste/_'dug la 'di skad ces brjod par bya'o/_/dge 'dun

[\[200a.1\]]dge 'dun gyi nang [#]du mtshams bu chung bcad cing dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par byas pa der dge slong rnams kyis 'dug la gso sbyong dang /_dgag dbye dang /_gsol ba dang /_gsol ba dang gnyis dang /_gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya'o/_/mtshams 
[\[200a.2\]]bu chung bcad cing dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par byas pa'i nang du dge slong thams cad kyis chos gos dag dang mi 'bral ba'i gnang ba sbyin pa ni gcig tu ma nges te/_gal te 'dod na ni sbyin par bya'o/_/de ni mtshams bu chung bcad cing dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par bya ba'i cho ga 
[\[200a.3\]]dang las so/_/gso sbyong gi gzhi las mthun pa'i gso sbyong gi gnas kyi skabs dge slong dag gis mtshams chen po bkrol te mtshams bu chung bcad cing dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par byas pa'i 'og tu/_yang gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas mtshams chen po gcad 
[\[200a.4\]]par[#] bya'o zhes gsungs pa ni 'di ltar bya ste/_snga ma bzhin du je dang por dge slong gnas pa dang gnyug mar gnas pa rnams kyis dpag tshad phyed dang gsum gyi mtha' tshun chad du mtshams chen po phyogs bzhi'i mtshan ma brtan po pha bong ngam/_rtsig pa 'am/_ka ba'am shing ljon pa 
[\[200a.5\]]'am/_ra ba'am bya skyibs sam/_lam po che'am khron pa la sogs pa'i mtshan ma brtan po gzugs par bya'o/_/de'i 'og tu gnas mal bsham pa byas nas gaN+DI brdungs te dris pa'i tshig gis dge slong rnams la yang dag par bsgo nas/_dge 'dun thams cad 'dug cing mthun 
[\[200a.6\]]par gyur pa la dge slong gnas pa dang gnyug mar gnas pa rnams kyis mtshams chen po'i phyogs bzhi'i mtshan ma brtan po btsugs pa dag brjod par bya'o/_/de'i 'og tu dge slong gcig gis gsol ba byas te las bya'o/_/'di ltar bya ste/_'dug la 'di skad ces brjod par 
[\[200a.7\]]bya'o/_/dge 'dun btsun pa [#]rnams gsan du gsol/_'di lta ste gnas 'di na dge slong gnas pa dang gnyug mar gnas pa rnams kyis mtshams chen po'i phyogs bzhi'i mtshan ma brtan po brjod pa [#]shar phyogs su ni pha bong gi mtshan ma 'am rtsig pa'i mtshan ma brtan po [#]lags/_lho 
[\[200b\]]
[\[200b.1\]]phyogs su ni ka ba'i mtshan ma 'am shing ljon pa'i mtshan ma brtan po lags/_nub phyogs su ni ra ba'i mtshan ma 'am bya skyibs kyi mtshan ma brtan po lags/_byang phyogs su ni lam po che'i mtshan ma 'am khron pa la sogs pa'i mtshan ma brtan po lags na/_gal te dge 'dun 
[\[200b.2\]]gyi [#]dus la bab cing bzod na dge 'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /_dge 'dun gyis mtshan ma 'di rnams kyi nang du gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas 'di lta ste/_gnas bsdu ba dang /_dge slong rnams bde ba la reg par gnas par bgyi ba'i slad du dge 'dun 
[\[200b.3\]]gyis grong [#]dang grong gi nye 'khor ma gtogs par dgon pa la thug [#]pa dang gnas mal la thug pa'i bar du mtshams chen po bcad do/_/'di ni gsol ba'o/_/las ni 'di ltar bya ste/_dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_'di lta ste gnas 'di na [#]dge slong gnas 
[\[200b.4\]]pa dang gnyug mar gnas pa rnams kyis mtshams chen po'i phyogs bzhi'i mtshan ma brtan po brjod pa shar phyogs su ni pha bong gi mtshan ma 'am rtsig pa'i mtshan ma brtan po lags/_lho phyogs su ni ka ba'i mtshan ma'am/_shing ljon pa'i mtshan ma brtan po lags/_nub 
[\[200b.5\]]phyogs su ni ra ba'i mtshan ma 'am bya skyibs kyi mtshan ma brtan po lags/_byang phyogs su ni lam po che'i mtshan ma 'am khron pa la sogs pa'i mtshan ma brtan po lags te/_de'i slad du dge 'dun gyis mtshan ma 'di rnams kyi nang du gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas 'di lta 
[\[200b.6\]]ste/_gnas bsdu ba dang /_dge slong rnams bde ba la reg par gnas par bgyi ba'i slad du grong dang grong gi nye 'khor ma gtogs par dgon pa la thug pa dang /_gnas mal la thug pa'i bar du mtshams chen po gcod na/_tshe dang ldan pa gang dag mtshan ma 'di rnams kyi nang du gso sbyong 
[\[200b.7\]]gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pa [#]'di lta ste/_gnas bsdu ba dang /_dge slong rnams bde ba la reg par gnas par bgyi ba'i slad du/_grong dang grong gi nye 'khor ma gtogs par [#]dgon pa la thug pa dang gnas mal la thug pa'i bar du mtshams chen po bcad par bzod pa de dag ni cang ma 
[\[201a\]]
[\[201a.1\]]gsung [#]shig_/gang dag mi bzod pa de dag ni gsungs shig_/dge 'dun gyis bzod cing gnang nas/_dge 'dun gyis mtshan ma 'di rnams kyi nang du gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas 'di lta ste/_gnas bsdu ba dang /_dge slong rnams bde ba 
[\[201a.2\]]la reg par gnas par bgyi ba'i slad du grong dang grong gi nye 'khor ma gtogs par dgon pa la thug pa dang /_gnas mal la thug pa'i bar du mtshams chen po bcad lags te/_'di ltar cang mi gsung bas [#]de de bzhin du 'dzin to/_/dge 'dun gyi [#]gang du mtshams chen po bcad pa der 
[\[201a.3\]]dge slong rnams kyis 'dug la gso sbyong dang /_dgag dbye dang /_gsol ba dang /_gsol ba dang gnyis dang /_gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya'o/_/mtshams bcad pa ni 'dun pa 'bul bas de dang mthun pa mi 'grub ste/_mtshams der gtogs pa'i dge slong thams cad kyi 
[\[201a.4\]]las[#] kyi gnas der 'du bar bya'o/_/de ni dge slong dag gis mtshams chen po bkrol te mtshams bu chung bcad cing dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par byas pa'i 'og tu yang dge slong gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas mtshams chen po bcad pa'i cho ga dang las so/_/gso 
[\[201a.5\]]sbyong gi gzhi las mthun pa'i gso sbyong gi gnas kyi skabs nas dge slong gang dag gis mtshams chen po bkrol te mtshams bu chung bcad cing dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par byas pa'i 'og tu yang gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas mtshams chen po bcad nas yang 
[\[201a.6\]]mtshams chen po'i nang du dge slong thams cad kyis chos gos dag dang mi 'bral ba'i gnang ba sbyin par bya'o zhes gsungs pa ni 'di ltar bya ste/_snga ma bzhin du gnas mal bsham pa byas nas gaN+DI brdungs te dris pa'i tshig gis dge slong rnams la yang dag par bsgo nas/_dge 'dun 
[\[201a.7\]]thams cad 'dug cing mthun par gyur pa la dge slong gcig gis gsol ba byas te las bya'o/_/'di ltar bya ste/_'dug la 'di skad ces brjod par bya'o/_/dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_'di lta ste gnas 'dir dge 'dun mthun pas gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas 
[\[201b\]]
[\[201b.1\]]kyi sdom pas mtshams chen po bcad pa las/_gal te dge 'dun gyi dus la bab cing bzod na dge 'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /_dge 'dun gyis 'di lta ste/_gnas bsdu ba dang /_dge slong rnams bde ba la reg par gnas par bgyi ba'i slad du mtshams chen po'i nang du 
[\[201b.2\]]dge slong thams cad kyi chos gos dag dang 'bral bar mi 'gyur ba'i gnang ba gsol to/_/'di ni gsol ba'o/_/las ni 'di ltar bya ste/_dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_'di lta ste gnas 'dir dge 'dun mthun pas gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas 
[\[201b.3\]]mtshams chen po bcad de/_de'i slad du dge 'dun gyis gnas [#]'di lta ste/_gnas bsdu ba dang /_dge slong rnams bde ba la reg par gnas par bgyi ba'i slad du mtshams chen po'i nang du dge slong thams cad kyi [#]chos gos dag dang 'bral bar mi 'gyur ba'i gnang ba gsol na/_tshe 
[\[201b.4\]]dang ldan pa gang dag 'di lta ste/_gnas bsdu ba dang /_dge slong rnams bde ba la reg par gnas par bgyi ba'i slad du mtshams chen po'i nang du dge slong thams cad kyi chos gos dag dang 'bral bar mi 'gyur ba'i gnang ba stsal bar [#]bzod pa de dag ni cang ma gsung shig_/gang 
[\[201b.5\]]dag mi bzod pa de dag ni gsungs shig_/dge 'dun gyis bzod cing gnang nas dge 'dun gyis mtshams chen po'i nang du dge slong thams cad kyi chos gos dag dang 'bral bar mi 'gyur ba'i gnang ba stsal lags te/_'di ltar cang mi gsung bas [#]de de bzhin du 'dzin to/_/dge 
[\[201b.6\]]'dun gyis gang du mtshams chen po'i nang du dge slong rnams kyi chos gos dag dang 'bral bar mi 'gyur ba'i gnang ba byin pa der dge slong rnams stod g.yogs smad g.yogs dang bcas pas 'dong bar bya ste/_'di la 'gyod par mi bya'o/_/de ni dge slong dag gis mtshams chen 
[\[201b.7\]]po bkrol te/_mtshams bu chung bcad cing dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par byas pa'i 'og tu yang gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas mtshams chen po bcad nas yang mtshams chen po'i nang du dge slong thams cad kyi chos gos dag dang mi 'bral ba'i gnang ba sbyin pa'i cho ga

[\[202a.3\]]'dun bkod de dge slong las byed pas mtshams gnyi gar shing ngam dbyug pa'am gseg [#]gam re ldes mnan nas gsol ba byas te las bya'o/_/'di ltar bya ste/_'dug la 'di skad ces brjod par bya'o/_/dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_'di lta ste gnas 'dir 
[\[202a.4\]]dge 'dun mthun pas gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas mtshams chen po bcad cing mtshams [#]bu chung bcad de dkyil 'khor par blo mthun [#]par bgyis pa/_gal te dge 'dun gyi dus la bab cing bzod na dge 'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /_dge 'dun gyis mtshams 
[\[202a.5\]]chen po gzhig cing mtshams bu chung yang gzhig ste dkyil 'khor pa dgrol bar mdzad do/_/'di ni gsol ba'o/_/las ni 'di ltar bya ste/_dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_'di lta ste gnas 'dir dge 'dun mthun pas gso sbyong gcig pa'i gnas kyi sdom pas mtshams 
[\[202a.6\]]chen po bcad cing mtshams [#]bu chung yang bcad de dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par bgyis pa de'i slad du dge 'dun gyis mtshams chen po gzhig cing mtshams bu chung yang gzhig ste dkyil 'khor ba dgrol bar mdzad na/_tshe dang ldan pa gang dag mtshams chen po gzhig cing mtshams bu chung

[\[202b.6\]]ldes mnan nas gsol ba byas te las bya'o/_/'di ltar bya ste/_'dug la 'di skad ces brjod par bya'o/_/dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/_'di lta ste/_gnas 'di na dge slong gnas pa dang gnyug mar gnas pa rnams kyis mtshams chen po'i phyogs bzhi'i mtshan

[\[203a.3\]]bsdu ba dang /_dge slong rnams bde ba la reg par gnas par bgyi ba'i slad du/_grong dang grong gi nye 'khor ma gtogs par dgon pa la thug pa dang /_gnas mal la thug pa'i bar du mtshams chen po bcad cing mtshams bu chung yang bcad de dkyil 'khor par blo mthun par

[\[257a.7\]]dbyar gyi gos ras chen 'gyed pa bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_mngag gzhug par [#]bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_mdzes 'chos bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge 'dun la rtsod pa byung ba mngon sum gyis 'dul ba'i gzu

[\[257b.1\]]bo bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge 'dun la rtsod pa byung ba gzu bos zhi bar ma nus na dge 'dun nam gzu bo rnams kyis rtsod pa de zhi bar byed pa'i gang zag gsal ba dang gsal ba'i yang gsal ba bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge 'dun la rtsod 
[\[257b.2\]]pa byung ba gang zag gsal ba [#]dang gsal ba'i [#]yang gsal bas zhi bar ma nus te dge 'dun la phul ba na dge 'dun gyis rtsod pa sbed pa'i gang zag bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge 'dun [#]rtsod pa byung ba'i tshe tshul shing 'brim pa'i [#]gang zag bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi 
[\[257b.3\]]las bya ba dang /_dge slong khang pa'i gzhi rung ba bstan par [#]gsol ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong gtsug lag khang gi gzhi rung ba bstan pa gsol ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong khyim sun 'byin pa la dge sbyong gi chos ma yin pa rnam pa mang pos gleng 
[\[257b.4\]]ba'i gang zag bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_rgan po dang nad pa la chos gos [#]snam sbyar dang 'bral bar mi 'gyur ba'i gnang ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong lag gi bla la lo drug tshun chad du stan gyi gnang ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba 
[\[257b.5\]]dang /_lhung bzed 'brel pa med par 'brim pa'i gang zag bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong dang dge slong ma skye bo [#]phal po che ma dad par rig par 'gyur ba de ltar khyim rnams su so sor go bar byed pa'i gang zag bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong ma'i 
[\[257b.6\]]ston pa bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_so sor bshags pa'i nyes pa spang ba'i phyir gang zag nags nyul ba bsko ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong dge 'dun gyi [#]gnas nas phyung ba la dge slong mas phyag bya ba'i 'os ma yin pa'i sdom pa sbyin pa la gsol ba dang 
[\[257b.7\]]gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong ma 'khor skyong nus pa la 'khor nye bar 'jog pa'i gnang ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong ma 'khor skyong nus pa dang lo bcu gnyis lon [#]pa la 'khor mang po nye bar 'jog pa'i gnang [#]ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_
[\[258a\]]
[\[258a.1\]]dge slong rgan po dang nad pa la 'khar ba gnang ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong rgan po dang na ba la dra ba gnang ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_gtsug lag khang mtshams tha dad pa gnyis kyi rnyed pa gcig tu bya ba la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba 
[\[258a.2\]]dang /_dge slong ma'i bslab pa'i sdom pa sbyin pa la gsol ba dang [#]gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong ma bsnyen par rdzogs par bya ba la [#]tshangs par spyod pa la nye bar gnas pa'i sdom pa sbyin pa la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong ma bu dang lhan cig khyim gcig na nyal ba'i gnang ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang 
[\[258a.3\]]gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dge slong ma [#]mu ge'i sdug bsngal byung ba'i tshe nye du rnams dang lhan cig 'brel ba'i nye bar spyod pa'i [#]gnang ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba dang /_dbyar gnas par khas blangs pa dge 'dun gyi tshad [#]la zhag bzhi bcu mtshams kyi phyi rol du 'gro ba'i gnang ba sbyin pa la 
[\[258a.4\]]gsol ba dang gnyis kyi las bya ba'o/[ #]/gsol ba dang bzhi'i las sum cu gang zhe na/_dge slong dang dge slong ma bsnyen par rdzogs par bya ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_mu stegs can rab tu 'byung bar 'dod pa zla ba bzhi'i bar du mkhan pos sbyar ba'i gos kyi [#]gnas pa [#]sbyin pa la gsol ba dang 
[\[258a.5\]]bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_dkyil 'khor pa dang /_mtshams chen po dang /_mtshams bu chung dgrol ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_gnas nas phyung ba bzod pa'i las byas pa mthun pa sbyin pa la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_mthun pa byin pa la [#]mthun pa'i gso sbyong sbyin pa la 
[\[258a.6\]]gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_nan tur gyi las bsdigs pa dang /_smad pa dang /_bskrad pa dang /_phyir 'gyed pa dang /_ma mthong bas gnas nas dbyung ba dang /_phyir mi 'chos pas gnas nas dbyung ba dang /_sdig pa can gyi lta ba'i rnam pa mi gtong bas gnas nas dbyung ba dang /[ #]gso sbyong 
[\[258a.7\]]gzhag pa dang /_dgag dbye gzhag par bya ba dag la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_de dag la bzod pa bya ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_bya ba dag la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /[ #]spo ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /[ #]gzhi nas spo ba sbyin pa la gsol 
[\[258b\]]
[\[258b.1\]]ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_gzhi nas mgu bar bya ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_yang gzhi nas bslang ste spo ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_yang gzhi nas bslang ste mgu bar bya ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_dge 'dun lhag ma'i ltung ba byung ba [#]la gsol 
[\[258b.2\]]ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_sra brkyang dbyung ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_dran pas 'dul ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_ma smyos pas 'dul ba sbyin pa la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_de'i ngo bo nyid tshol du gzhug pa sbyin [#]la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_dge 
[\[258b.3\]]'dun gyi dbyen byed pa bzlog pa'i phyir bsgo ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_de'i [#]rjes su phyogs pa bzlog pa'i phyir bsgo ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_khyim sun 'byin pa bzlog pa'i phyir bsgo ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_bka' blo mi bde ba bzlog pa'i phyir bsgo ba la gsol ba dang 
[\[258b.4\]]bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_dge slong dang dge tshul sdig pa can gyi lta ba'i rnam pa mi gtong ba bzlog pa'i phyir bsgo ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_gnas nas phyung ba'i rjes su phyogs pa'i dge slong ma bzlog pa'i phyir bsgo ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_dge slong ma dkon mchog gtong ba 
[\[258b.5\]]bzlog [#]pa'i phyir bsgo ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_dge slong ma 'thab krol byed pa bzlog pa'i phyir bsgo ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_dge slong ma sde zhing gnas pa bzlog [#]pa'i phyir bsgo ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_dge slong ma sde zhing gnas su 'dzud pa bzlog pa'i phyir bsgo 
[\[258b.6\]]ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_dge tshul sdig pa can gyi lta ba'i rnam pa mi gtong ba bsnyil ba la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba dang /_pham par 'gyur ba'i ltung ba byung ba la bslab pa sbyin pa la gsol ba dang bzhi'i las bya ba'o/_/las rnams kyi bsdu ba'i tshigs su bcad pa ni 'di dag yin te/_gsang dang 
[\[258b.7\]]nang du dri phyir dang /_/gso sbyong de la ltung bcas dang /_/de la dge 'dun yid gnyis dang /_/rtsod dang nges nyid ma yin dang /_/de yi don du lnga po dag_/thams cad dgag dbyer gtogs pa yin/_/dbyar gnas khas blangs [#]nyid dang ni/_/shi ba'i rdzas dang nor 'dra dang /_/chos gos sra brkyang gtad pa dang /_/chos gos sbyin par 
[\[259a\]]
[\[259a.1\]]bya ba dang /_/gnas ngan len ni brjod pa dang /_/grangs su gzhug pa bzhi dag dang /_/khyim gyi sdom dang de gzhig dang /_/lhung bzed khas dbub bya ba dang /_/de nyid bzlog par bya ba dag_/gsol ba 'ba' zhig las yin no/_/gnas dang mtshams ni gsum po dang /_/mi 'bral smyos dang dgag dbye dang /_/
[\[259a.2\]]gnas mal stobs par byed pa dang /_/rung ba'i [#]sa gzhi bsgo ba dang /_/sra brkyang dang ni de 'ding dang /_/spong dang lag gi bla dang ni/_/gnas khang bsko la sogs pa dang /_/gzu dang gsal dang sbed pa dang /_/tshul shing 'brim par byed pa dang /_/khang pa khang chen bstan pa dang /_/gleng dang 'bral bar mi 'gyur dang /_/
[\[259a.3\]]stan [#]dang lhung bzed 'brim pa dang /_/ma dad go bar bya ba dang /_/ston pa dang ni nags nyul dang /_/phyag mi bya dang mang phyir dang /_/'khar ba dra ba'i phyir dang ni/_/rnyed pa gcig tu bya phyir dang /_/bslab pa sbyin dang nye [#]gnas dang /_/bu dang nye du'i phyir 'gro ba/_/gsol ba [#]gnyis kyi las yin no/_/bsnyen 
[\[259a.4\]]par rdzogs par bya ba dang /_/mu stegs gnas dang mtshams dgrol dang /_/mthun sbyin gso sbyong bdun po dag_/brgya [#]dang dran dang ma smyos dang /_/de nyid tshol du gzhug pa dang /_/bsgo bar bya ba rnams dang ni/_/nyams pa sbyin par bya ba dag_/gsol dang bzhi yi las 
[\[259a.5\]]yin[#] no/_/las brgya rtsa gcig pa/_'phags pa gzhi thams cad yod par smra ba'i 'dul ba 'dzin pa chen po bram ze'i btsun pa slob dpon yon tan 'od bdag dang gzhan gyi grub pa'i mtha' rgya mtsho'i pha rol du son pa/_bde bar gshegs pa'i chos 'dul ba bdud rtsi'i nying khus shes 
[\[259a.6\]]rab kyi dbang po rgyas pa bde bar gshegs pa'i bka' dam pa nan tan snying por bsgrubs pa/[ #]sde pa thams cad kyi 'dul ba'i sde snod mtha' dag gi gtso bor [#]gyur pa/_'phags pa gzhi thams cad yod par smra ba'i 'dul ba kun las btus pa rdzogs so//_//dbang phyug dam pa'i mnga' bdag dpal lha 
[\[259a.7\]]btsan po'i bka' lung gis 'phags pa gzhi thams cad yod par smra ba'i 'dul ba 'dzin pa kha che'i bye brag tu smra ba'i slob dpon dzi na mi tra dang /_zhu chen gyi lo tsA ba ban+de klu'i rgyal mtshan gyis bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa/_'di la sh+lo ka [#]ni sum stong bdun brgya yod/_bam po ni dgur byas so//_//[#]
